		multiply_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_shangdiism = 100 }
		divide_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_islamism = aotsm_demo_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_islamism = 100 }
		divide_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_buddism = aotsm_demo_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_buddism = 100 }
		divide_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_induism = aotsm_demo_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_induism = 100 }
		divide_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_judaism = aotsm_demo_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_judaism = 100 }
		divide_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_konfucianstvo = aotsm_demo_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_konfucianstvo = 100 }
		divide_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_sintoism = aotsm_demo_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_sintoism = 100 }
		divide_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_aztec_paganism = aotsm_demo_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_aztec_paganism = 100 }
		divide_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_paganism = aotsm_demo_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_paganism = 100 }
		divide_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_atheism = aotsm_demo_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_atheism = 100 }
		divide_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_sikhism = aotsm_demo_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_sikhism = 100 }
	}
	aotsm_demo_refresh_state = yes # Same leader calculation; does not change shares.
}

# State scope. DONOR/RECIPIENT are registry keys, POINTS are percentage points.
# Example: aotsm_demo_transfer_culture = { DONOR = example RECIPIENT = example POINTS = 0.1 }
aotsm_demo_transfer_culture = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = aotsm_demo_initialized_v1
			check_variable = { var = global.aotsm_demo_c_id_$DONOR$ value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = global.aotsm_demo_c_id_$RECIPIENT$ value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = aotsm_demo_c_$DONOR$ value = 0 compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { aotsm_demo_transfer = $POINTS$ }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = aotsm_demo_transfer min = 0 max = aotsm_demo_c_$DONOR$ }
		subtract_from_variable = { aotsm_demo_c_$DONOR$ = aotsm_demo_transfer }
		add_to_variable = { aotsm_demo_c_$RECIPIENT$ = aotsm_demo_transfer }
		aotsm_demo_rebuild_lists = yes
		aotsm_demo_refresh_state = yes
		owner = { aotsm_demo_refresh_country = yes }
		force_update_map_mode = { mapmode = religion_map_mode }
		force_update_map_mode = { mapmode = aotsm_culture_map_mode }
	}
}

# State scope. DONOR/RECIPIENT are registry keys, POINTS are percentage points.
# Example: aotsm_demo_transfer_religion = { DONOR = example RECIPIENT = example POINTS = 0.1 }
aotsm_demo_transfer_religion = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = aotsm_demo_initialized_v1
			check_variable = { var = global.aotsm_demo_r_id_$DONOR$ value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = global.aotsm_demo_r_id_$RECIPIENT$ value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = aotsm_demo_r_$DONOR$ value = 0 compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { aotsm_demo_transfer = $POINTS$ }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = aotsm_demo_transfer min = 0 max = aotsm_demo_r_$DONOR$ }
		subtract_from_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_$DONOR$ = aotsm_demo_transfer }
		add_to_variable = { aotsm_demo_r_$RECIPIENT$ = aotsm_demo_transfer }
		aotsm_demo_rebuild_lists = yes
		aotsm_demo_refresh_state = yes
		owner = { aotsm_demo_refresh_country = yes }
		force_update_map_mode = { mapmode = religion_map_mode }
		force_update_map_mode = { mapmode = aotsm_culture_map_mode }
	}
}
